    tests/test_server.py
    """Tests for server/app.py. The search, explainer and formalizer are faked, so no Lean or model is needed."""

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

import server.app as srv
from mathfinder import cli
from mathfinder.formalize import Formalization
from mathfinder.planner import Plan
from mathfinder.search import SearchResult


@pytest.fixture
def client(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "RUNS_DIR", tmp_path)  # run folders go to a temporary place
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.explainer, "explain", lambda theorem, plan, code: "## Proof\nby simp")
    srv.RUNS.clear()
    return TestClient(srv.app)


def fake_search_that_proves(statement, rounds, on_event, session):
    on_event({"kind": "start", "depth": 0, "theorem": statement})
    on_event({"kind": "lemma_proved", "depth": 0, "name": "key", "statement": ": True", "feedback": "hammer"})
    on_event({"kind": "done", "depth": 0, "lean_code": "theorem main ...", "plan": "simp it"})
    return SearchResult(True, "theorem main ...", Plan("simp it", [], "simp"), 1, "verified")


def watch_until_finished(client, run_id):
    with client.websocket_connect(f"/ws/{run_id}") as ws:
        events = []
        while not events or events[-1]["kind"] != "finished":
            events.append(ws.receive_json())
    return events


def test_formalize_endpoint(client, monkeypatch):
    f = Formalization("x", "(n : ℕ) : n = n", "For all n, n = n.", True, "same")
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.formalizer, "formalize", lambda latex: f)
    data = client.post("/api/formalize", json={"latex": "$n = n$"}).json()
    assert data["statement"] == "(n : ℕ) : n = n" and data["ok"] and data["faithful"]


def test_a_search_streams_its_events_and_ends_with_the_result(client, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.engine, "search", fake_search_that_proves)
    run_id = client.post("/api/sessions", json={"statement": "(n : ℕ) : n = n"}).json()["id"]

    events = watch_until_finished(client, run_id)
    kinds = [e["kind"] for e in events]
    assert kinds == ["start", "lemma_proved", "done", "explaining", "finished"]
    result = events[-1]["result"]
    assert result["proved"] and result["explanation"] == "## Proof\nby simp"

    status = client.get(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}").json()
    assert status["status"] == "proved"
    assert (cli.RUNS_DIR / status["result"]["folder"].split("/")[-1] / "proof.lean").exists()


def test_commands_reach_the_session_while_it_waits_for_an_answer(client, monkeypatch):
    def fake_search_that_asks(statement, rounds, on_event, session):
        on_event({"kind": "ask_user", "depth": 0, "question": "Any idea?"})
        answer = session.ask("Any idea?")
        on_event({"kind": "user_answer", "depth": 0, "text": answer})
        return SearchResult(False, message=f"hints were {session.hints()}")

    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.engine, "search", fake_search_that_asks)
    run_id = client.post("/api/sessions", json={"statement": "t", "hints": ["start hint"]}).json()["id"]
    assert client.post(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}/hint", json={"text": "try cases"}).json() == {"ok": True}
    with client.websocket_connect(f"/ws/{run_id}") as ws:
        assert ws.receive_json()["kind"] == "ask_user"
        assert client.get(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}").json()["question"] == "Any idea?"
        client.post(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}/answer", json={"text": "use induction"})
        assert ws.receive_json() == {"kind": "user_answer", "depth": 0, "text": "use induction"}
        final = ws.receive_json()
    assert final["kind"] == "finished" and final["status"] == "not_proved"
    assert final["result"]["message"] == "hints were ['start hint', 'try cases']"

  6. Create the demo tool.

    tools/make_demo.py
    """Turn a real trace into web/demo_events.json, so the web page can replay it.

  uv run python tools/make_demo.py runs/<folder>/trace.jsonl

Then open http://localhost:8765/#demo: the page shows that search as if it were live,
with no Lean and no model needed. Handy for working on the page's look.
"""

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

WEB_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "web"


def main() -> None:
    trace = Path(sys.argv[1])
    events = [json.loads(line) for line in trace.read_text().splitlines() if line.strip()]
    keep = [{k: v for k, v in e.items() if k != "time"} for e in events if e["kind"] not in ("meta", "result")]
    out = WEB_DIR / "demo_events.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(keep, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))
    print(f"wrote {len(keep)} events to {out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Common errors

  • The page is unstyled, or maths shows as raw $...$: no internet, so the CDN files didn't load. Everything else still works.
  • {"detail":"Not Found"} at /: the app.mount(...) line is missing, or web/index.html isn't there.
  • API calls return HTML instead of JSON: the mount comes before the routes. Move it to the very end of app.py.
  • The tree never fills in: open the browser's developer console (⌥⌘J in Chrome). A red error there usually names the line in app.js.
  • #demo shows nothing: web/demo_events.json is missing. Run step 7.

M11: From a proof to a better proof

What and why. The search stops at the first proof Lean accepts, and that proof is often clumsy: three helper lemmas where one line would do, or a black-box nlinarith where a two-line argument would actually show why. Once we have one verified proof, we can afford to look for a better one. "Better" depends on what you want, so there are three metrics (scoring rules; lower is better). Each one combines counts taken from the Lean code: steps (tactic lines), lemmas (helpers besides main) and heavy (brute-force automation calls):

MetricScorePrefers
shortsteps + lemmasthe fewest lines
readablesteps + lemmas + 5 × heavyexplicit reasoning over black-box automation
few_lemmas10 × lemmas + stepsone self-contained proof

The optimizer asks the model for a few rewrites, checks them all in one Lean run, and keeps the cheapest only if it beats the original and passes the strict final check. The search budget is bounded (one model call, one batch, one final check), and the original proof is never lost.

  1. Create the optimizer.

    mathfinder/optimize.py
    """The optimizer: once we HAVE a verified proof, look for a better one.

"Better" depends on what you care about, so there are several scoring rules
(METRICS). Lower is better. Each one combines three counts taken from the Lean code:

  steps   - tactic lines (how long the proof is)
  lemmas  - helper lemmas besides `main`
  heavy   - calls to brute-force automation (nlinarith, aesop, decide, ...) that
            prove things without showing WHY

The search is bounded: one model call proposes a few rewrites of the whole proof,
Lean checks them all in one run, and the best one is kept only if it scores lower
AND passes the strict final verification. The original proof is never lost.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass

from mathfinder import llm
from mathfinder.lean import Decl, check_decls, verify_final
from mathfinder.planner import Plan
from mathfinder.prover import parse_proofs
from mathfinder.search import NAMESPACE, assemble

CANDIDATES = 4
HEAVY = ("nlinarith", "polyrith", "aesop", "simp_all", "decide", "exact?")

METRICS = {
    "short": lambda s: s["steps"] + s["lemmas"],  # fewest lines overall
    "readable": lambda s: s["steps"] + s["lemmas"] + 5 * s["heavy"],  # avoid black-box automation
    "few_lemmas": lambda s: 10 * s["lemmas"] + s["steps"],  # one self-contained proof
}

DESCRIPTIONS = {
    "short": "as SHORT as possible (fewest tactic lines)",
    "readable": "as READABLE as possible: prefer explicit steps (rw, calc, have, exact lemma) over brute-force "
    "automation like nlinarith, aesop, decide or simp_all",
    "few_lemmas": "self-contained: one proof of the main theorem, using `have` instead of separate lemmas",
}

SYSTEM = """You are an expert in Lean 4 and Mathlib who polishes proofs that already work.
You get a theorem and a VERIFIED proof of it (possibly split into helper lemmas).
Write alternative proofs of the main theorem alone, each one a single tactic script
(no separate lemmas: inline them with `have` if needed). Use current Mathlib names.
Never use sorry, admit or axiom. Write ONLY the tactic script that goes after `:= by`.
Reply with ONLY a JSON object inside a ```json block: {"proofs": ["<tactic script>", ...]}"""


@dataclass
class Optimization:
    lean_code: str  # the best verified proof (maybe the original)
    plan: Plan
    metric: str
    original: dict  # stats of the original proof, including "cost"
    best: dict  # stats of the chosen proof, including "cost"
    tried: int  # how many candidates Lean checked
    improved: bool


def proof_stats(lean_code: str, metric: str = "short") -> dict:
    """Count steps, lemmas and heavy automation in an assembled proof file, and score it."""
    lines = lean_code.split("\n")
    steps = sum(1 for line in lines if line.startswith(" ") and line.strip())  # indented = inside a proof
    lemmas = sum(1 for line in lines if line.startswith("theorem ")) - 1  # every theorem except main
    heavy = sum(line.count(word) for line in lines for word in HEAVY)
    stats = {"steps": steps, "lemmas": max(lemmas, 0), "heavy": heavy}
    stats["cost"] = METRICS[metric](stats)
    return stats


def build_prompt(theorem: str, lean_code: str, metric: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"Theorem:\n  theorem main {theorem}\n\n"
        f"A verified proof:\n```lean\n{lean_code}\n```\n\n"
        f"Give {CANDIDATES} different proofs of `main` that are {DESCRIPTIONS[metric]}."
    )


def optimize(theorem: str, lean_code: str, plan: Plan, metric: str = "short", on_event=None) -> Optimization:
    """Try to find a better verified proof than `lean_code` under `metric`."""

    def emit(kind, **data):
        if on_event:
            on_event({"kind": kind, "depth": 0, **data})

    original = proof_stats(lean_code, metric)
    keep = Optimization(lean_code, plan, metric, original, original, 0, False)
    emit("optimize_start", metric=metric, cost=original["cost"])
    try:
        proofs = parse_proofs(llm.complete_json(SYSTEM, build_prompt(theorem, lean_code, metric)))
    except llm.LLMError:
        proofs = []
    if not proofs:
        emit("optimized", improved=False, before=original, after=original, tried=0)
        return keep

    results = check_decls([Decl(f"candidate_{i}", theorem, p) for i, p in enumerate(proofs)])
    scored = []
    for i, proof in enumerate(proofs):
        if results[f"candidate_{i}"].ok:
            candidate_plan = Plan(f"Optimized ({metric})", [], proof)
            code = assemble(theorem, candidate_plan)
            scored.append((proof_stats(code, metric)["cost"], i, code, candidate_plan))
        emit("optimize_candidate", ok=results[f"candidate_{i}"].ok, proof=proof)

    for cost, _, code, candidate_plan in sorted(scored):  # cheapest first
        if cost >= original["cost"]:
            break  # nothing cheaper is left
        if verify_final(code, f"{NAMESPACE}.main").ok:
            best = proof_stats(code, metric)
            emit("optimized", improved=True, before=original, after=best, tried=len(proofs))
            return Optimization(code, candidate_plan, metric, original, best, len(proofs), True)

    emit("optimized", improved=False, before=original, after=original, tried=len(proofs))
    keep.tried = len(proofs)
    return keep

    METRICS is a dictionary of lambda functions, so adding a new scoring rule is one line. proof_stats reads the assembled file: indented lines are tactic steps (theorem headers aren't indented), lines starting with theorem are lemmas plus main, and words from HEAVY are counted wherever they appear.

    Reusing parts: candidates are checked with check_decls (M1), cleaned with the prover's parse_proofs (M4b), assembled with assemble and verified with verify_final (M4c), exactly like a normal search result. sorted(scored) sorts the (cost, index, code, plan) tuples by cost first. The loop breaks as soon as a candidate isn't cheaper than the original, because nothing after it in sorted order can be.

  2. Create its tests.

    tests/test_optimize.py
    """Tests for mathfinder/optimize.py."""

import json

import pytest

from mathfinder import llm
from mathfinder import optimize as opt
from mathfinder.planner import Lemma, Plan
from mathfinder.search import assemble

AMGM = "(a b : ℝ) : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 ≥ 2 * a * b"
LONG_PLAN = Plan(
    "Two lemmas, then linarith.",
    [
        Lemma("sq_diff", "(a b : ℝ) : (a - b) ^ 2 = a ^ 2 - 2 * a * b + b ^ 2", "", status="proved", proof="ring"),
        Lemma("sq_diff_nonneg", "(a b : ℝ) : 0 ≤ (a - b) ^ 2", "", status="proved", proof="positivity"),
    ],
    "have h1 := sq_diff a b\nhave h2 := sq_diff_nonneg a b\nlinarith",
)
LONG_CODE = assemble(AMGM, LONG_PLAN)


def test_proof_stats_counts_steps_lemmas_and_heavy_tactics():
    assert opt.proof_stats(LONG_CODE) == {"steps": 5, "lemmas": 2, "heavy": 0, "cost": 7}
    one_liner = assemble(AMGM, Plan("", [], "nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a - b)]"))
    assert opt.proof_stats(one_liner, "short") == {"steps": 1, "lemmas": 0, "heavy": 1, "cost": 1}
    assert opt.proof_stats(one_liner, "readable")["cost"] == 6  # 1 step + 5 for the heavy nlinarith


def test_metric_changes_which_proof_wins():
    one_liner = assemble(AMGM, Plan("", [], "nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a - b)]"))
    assert opt.proof_stats(one_liner, "short")["cost"] < opt.proof_stats(LONG_CODE, "short")["cost"]
    assert opt.proof_stats(one_liner, "readable")["cost"] < opt.proof_stats(LONG_CODE, "readable")["cost"]
    assert opt.proof_stats(one_liner, "few_lemmas")["cost"] < opt.proof_stats(LONG_CODE, "few_lemmas")["cost"]


def test_prompt_names_the_goal_of_the_metric():
    assert "READABLE" in opt.build_prompt(AMGM, LONG_CODE, "readable")


def test_no_candidates_keeps_the_original(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setitem(llm.BACKENDS, "fake", lambda s, p: '```json\n{"proofs": []}\n```')
    monkeypatch.setenv("MATHFINDER_LLM", "fake")
    result = opt.optimize(AMGM, LONG_CODE, LONG_PLAN)
    assert not result.improved and result.lean_code == LONG_CODE


@pytest.mark.lean
def test_a_shorter_verified_proof_replaces_the_original(monkeypatch):
    answers = {"proofs": ["linarith", "nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a - b)]"]}  # the first one is wrong
    monkeypatch.setitem(llm.BACKENDS, "fake", lambda s, p: "```json\n" + json.dumps(answers) + "\n```")
    monkeypatch.setenv("MATHFINDER_LLM", "fake")
    events = []
    result = opt.optimize(AMGM, LONG_CODE, LONG_PLAN, on_event=events.append)

    assert result.improved and result.tried == 2
    assert "nlinarith [sq_nonneg (a - b)]" in result.lean_code
    assert (result.original["cost"], result.best["cost"]) == (7, 1)
    assert [e["ok"] for e in events if e["kind"] == "optimize_candidate"] == [False, True]

    The fake-model Lean test offers one wrong rewrite (linarith, which Lean rejects) and one right one. The right one replaces a two-lemma proof, and the cost drops from 7 to 1.

  3. Optimize on the command line too. Replace the whole file:

    mathfinder/cli.py
    """Command line.

  uv run python -m mathfinder prove "For every integer $n$, $6 \\mid n^3 - n$."
  uv run python -m mathfinder prove --lean "(n : ℤ) : 6 ∣ n ^ 3 - n"

With LaTeX, you first see the Lean statement, its back-translation and the
judge's opinion, and nothing is searched until you approve.
Every run gets a folder runs/<date-time>/ with trace.jsonl (everything that happened);
a proved theorem also gets proof.lean + explanation.md there. After the first proof,
the optimizer looks for a better one (--metric short | readable | few_lemmas).
With --interactive you can type hints and commands while the search runs.

  uv run python -m mathfinder export      builds training data in data/ from all traces
"""

import argparse
import select
import sys
import threading
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from mathfinder import PROJECT_ROOT
from mathfinder.collab import Session
from mathfinder.explain import explain
from mathfinder.formalize import formalize
from mathfinder.lean import HEADER, statement_errors
from mathfinder.llm import LLMError
from mathfinder.optimize import METRICS, optimize
from mathfinder.search import search
from mathfinder.traces import TraceLogger, chain, export

RUNS_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "runs"
DATA_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "data"

SYMBOL = {"lemma_proved": "✓", "lemma_refuted": "✗", "lemma_stuck": "…"}


def print_event(event: dict) -> None:
    kind = event["kind"]
    if event.get("depth", 0) > 0:  # events from a sub-search are indented under their lemma
        indent = "        " * event["depth"]
        if kind in SYMBOL:
            print(f"{indent}{SYMBOL[kind]} {event['name']} : {event['statement']}   [{event['feedback'][:80]}]")
        elif kind == "plan_start":
            print(f"{indent}▶ sub-plan: {event['sketch']}")
        elif kind == "done":
            print(f"{indent}✓ sub-search proved it")
        sys.stdout.flush()
        return
    if kind == "start":
        print(f"\nTheorem: {event['theorem']}")
    elif kind == "subsearch_start":
        print(f"      ↳ {event['name']} is stuck: starting a sub-search on it")
    elif kind == "refuted":
        print(f"✗ The statement is FALSE. Counterexample: {event['counterexample']}")
    elif kind == "round":
        print(f"\n— round {event['number']}: asking the planner —")
    elif kind == "plan_rejected":
        print(f"  ✗ plan doesn't fit together: {event['sketch']}\n      {event['reason'][:160]}")
    elif kind == "plan_start":
        print(f"  ▶ plan: {event['sketch']}  (lemmas: {', '.join(event['lemmas']) or 'none'})")
    elif kind in SYMBOL:
        print(f"      {SYMBOL[kind]} {event['name']} : {event['statement']}   [{event['feedback'][:80]}]")
    elif kind == "plan_failed":
        print(f"    plan failed (score {event['score']:.2f})")
    elif kind == "done":
        print(f"\n✓ VERIFIED by Lean. Plan: {event['plan']}\n")
        print(event["lean_code"])
    elif kind == "gave_up":
        print("\nNo verified proof found.")
    elif kind == "ask_user":
        print(f"\n❓ {event['question']}\n   (type your answer and press Enter)")
    elif kind == "user_answer":
        print(f"  ← thanks, trying again with your idea: {event['text']}")
    elif kind == "no_answer":
        print("  (no answer)")
    elif kind == "stopped":
        print(f"\n■ Stopped: {event['reason']}")
    sys.stdout.flush()  # show progress immediately, even when output is piped to a file


def approve(latex: str, auto_yes: bool = False, ask=input) -> str | None:
    """Formalize `latex`, show it, and return the statement the user approved (None = no)."""
    print("Formalizing (LaTeX → Lean)...")
    f = formalize(latex)
    if not f.ok:
        print("Could not produce a statement Lean accepts:\n  " + f.errors[0][:400])
        return None
    print(f"\nFormal statement:  theorem main {f.statement}")
    print(f"Back-translation:  {f.back_translation}")
    print(f"Judge:             {'same meaning' if f.faithful else 'MEANING MAY DIFFER'}: {f.judgment}")

    statement = f.statement
    if auto_yes and f.faithful:
        return statement
    if auto_yes:
        print("(--yes ignored: the judge thinks the meaning may differ, so please look.)")
    while True:
        try:
            answer = ask("\nProve this statement? [y]es / [n]o / [e]dit: ").strip().lower()
        except EOFError:  # no keyboard attached (e.g. running in a script)
            return None
        if answer == "y":
            return statement
        if answer == "n":
            return None
        if answer == "e":
            new = ask("Lean statement (what comes after `theorem main`): ").strip()
            errors = statement_errors(new)
            if errors:
                print("Lean rejected it: " + errors[0][:300])
            else:
                statement = new
                print(f"Now:  theorem main {statement}")


COMMANDS = """While it runs you can type:
  hint <text>                     e.g.  hint try induction on n
  lemma <name> <lean statement>   e.g.  lemma key (a b : ℝ) : 0 ≤ (a - b) ^ 2
  reject <lemma name>             plans using that lemma are dropped
  pin <approach>                  "keep going in this direction"
  pause | resume | stop
When the search asks you a question, just type your answer."""


def handle_command(session: Session, line: str) -> str:
    """Apply one typed line to the session. Returns a short confirmation to print."""
    line = line.strip()
    word, _, rest = line.partition(" ")  # "hint try x" -> ("hint", " ", "try x")
    if session.question is not None and word not in ("pause", "resume", "stop"):
        session.answer(line)  # a question is open: the whole line is the answer
        return "answer sent"
    if word == "hint" and rest:
        session.add_hint(rest)
        return f"hint added: {rest}"
    if word == "lemma" and " " in rest:
        name, statement = rest.split(" ", 1)
        session.add_lemma(name, statement)
        return f"lemma suggested: {name}"
    if word == "reject" and rest:
        session.reject_lemma(rest)
        return f"rejected: {rest}"
    if word == "pin" and rest:
        session.pin(rest)
        return f"pinned: {rest}"
    if word == "pause":
        session.pause()
        return "paused (type resume to continue)"
    if word == "resume":
        session.resume()
        return "resumed"
    if word == "stop":
        session.stop()
        return "stopping..."
    return "unknown command. " + COMMANDS


def interactive_search(statement: str, hints: list[str], rounds: int, on_event=print_event):
    """Run the search in a background thread while this thread reads your commands.
    Returns (result, every hint used, including the ones you typed)."""
    session = Session(statement)
    for h in hints:
        session.add_hint(h)
    box = {}
    worker = threading.Thread(
        target=lambda: box.setdefault("result", search(statement, rounds=rounds, on_event=on_event, session=session)),
        daemon=True,  # don't keep Python alive just for this thread
    )
    worker.start()
    print(COMMANDS)
    keyboard_open = True
    while worker.is_alive():
        if not keyboard_open:
            worker.join(0.5)
            continue
        ready, _, _ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], 0.5)  # wait up to 0.5 s for a typed line
        if ready:
            line = sys.stdin.readline()
            if line == "":  # end of input (Ctrl-D, or a pipe that closed)
                keyboard_open = False
            elif line.strip():
                print(f"  [{handle_command(session, line)}]")
    return box["result"], session.hints()


def new_run_dir() -> Path:
    """A fresh folder for this run: runs/<date-time>/ (with -2, -3, ... if that name is taken)."""
    base = RUNS_DIR / datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    folder, n = base, 1
    while folder.exists():
        n += 1
        folder = base.with_name(f"{base.name}-{n}")
    folder.mkdir(parents=True)
    return folder


def save_run(folder: Path, theorem: str, lean_code: str, explanation: str) -> Path:
    """Save a proved theorem into its run folder: theorem.txt, proof.lean, explanation.md."""
    (folder / "theorem.txt").write_text(theorem + "\n")
    (folder / "proof.lean").write_text(HEADER + lean_code)  # a complete file Lean can check on its own
    (folder / "explanation.md").write_text(explanation + "\n")
    return folder


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="mathfinder")
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    prove = commands.add_parser("prove", help="search for a proof")
    prove.add_argument("latex", nargs="?", help="the theorem in LaTeX (in quotes)")
    prove.add_argument("--lean", help='skip formalization: a Lean statement, e.g. "(n : ℕ) : n + 0 = n"')
    prove.add_argument("--yes", action="store_true", help="don't ask for approval if the judge is happy")
    prove.add_argument("--hint", action="append", default=[], help="a hint for the planner (repeatable)")
    prove.add_argument("--rounds", type=int, default=3)
    prove.add_argument("--no-explain", action="store_true", help="skip the plain-math explanation")
    prove.add_argument("--metric", choices=sorted(METRICS), default="short", help="what 'better proof' means")
    prove.add_argument("--no-optimize", action="store_true", help="keep the first proof found")
    prove.add_argument("--interactive", action="store_true", help="type hints and commands while it searches")
    export_cmd = commands.add_parser("export", help="build training data from all runs/*/trace.jsonl")
    export_cmd.add_argument("--out", default=str(DATA_DIR), help="output folder (default: data/)")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    if args.command == "export":
        counts = export(RUNS_DIR, Path(args.out))
        for name, count in counts.items():
            print(f"{name:12} {count:5} rows  -> {Path(args.out) / (name + '.jsonl')}")
        return 0

    if args.command == "prove":
        if args.lean:
            statement = args.lean
        elif args.latex:
            statement = approve(args.latex, args.yes)
            if statement is None:
                print("Not searching.")
                return 1
        else:
            parser.error("give a theorem in LaTeX, or --lean STATEMENT")
        folder = new_run_dir()
        logger = TraceLogger(folder, args.latex or statement)
        on_event = chain(print_event, logger)  # print AND log every event
        if args.interactive:
            result, hints_used = interactive_search(statement, args.hint, args.rounds, on_event)
        else:
            result = search(statement, hints=args.hint, rounds=args.rounds, on_event=on_event)
            hints_used = args.hint
        logger.finish(result.proved, hints_used)
        print(f"\nResult: {'PROVED' if result.proved else 'not proved'} ({result.message})")
        print(f"Trace: {logger.path}")
        if not result.proved:
            return 1
        lean_code, plan = result.lean_code, result.plan
        if not args.no_optimize:
            print(f"\nLooking for a better proof (metric: {args.metric})...")
            better = optimize(statement, lean_code, plan, args.metric, on_event)
            print(f"  cost {better.original['cost']} → {better.best['cost']}"
                  + ("  (better proof found)" if better.improved else "  (kept the first proof)"))
            if better.improved:
                lean_code, plan = better.lean_code, better.plan
                print("\n" + lean_code)
        explanation = ""
        if not args.no_explain:
            print("\nWriting the explanation...")
            try:
                explanation = explain(args.latex or statement, plan, lean_code)
                print("\n" + explanation)
            except LLMError as e:  # the proof is still valid; only the write-up failed
                print(f"(could not write an explanation: {e})")
        print(f"\nSaved to {save_run(folder, args.latex or statement, lean_code, explanation)}")
        return 0
    return 2

    What changed: import METRICS, optimize, the new flags --metric (with choices=sorted(METRICS), argparse rejects anything else) and --no-optimize, and the end of main. After a proof is found, it optimizes, and the explanation and saved file use the chosen proof (lean_code, plan), not necessarily the first one.

    """Tests for mathfinder/cli.py: approval, explanation, saving, tracing, typed commands. Lean and the model are faked."""

import json

import pytest

from mathfinder import cli
from mathfinder.collab import Session
from mathfinder.formalize import Formalization
from mathfinder.llm import LLMError
from mathfinder.optimize import Optimization
from mathfinder.planner import Plan
from mathfinder.search import SearchResult


@pytest.fixture
def fake_formalize(monkeypatch):
    def install(faithful=True):
        result = Formalization("latex", "(n : ℤ) : 6 ∣ n ^ 3 - n", "For all integers n, 6 | n^3 - n.", faithful, "ok")
        monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "formalize", lambda latex: result)

    return install


def scripted_user(*answers):
    """Stands in for input(): gives the answers in order."""
    queue = list(answers)
    return lambda question: queue.pop(0)


def test_yes_returns_the_statement(fake_formalize):
    fake_formalize()
    assert cli.approve("latex", ask=scripted_user("y")) == "(n : ℤ) : 6 ∣ n ^ 3 - n"


def test_no_returns_none(fake_formalize):
    fake_formalize()
    assert cli.approve("latex", ask=scripted_user("n")) is None


def test_edit_is_checked_by_lean_before_it_is_used(fake_formalize, monkeypatch):
    fake_formalize()
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "statement_errors", lambda s: ["unknown identifier 'oops'"] if "oops" in s else [])
    user = scripted_user("e", "(n : ℤ) : oops", "e", "(n : ℕ) : 6 ∣ n ^ 3 - n", "y")
    assert cli.approve("latex", ask=user) == "(n : ℕ) : 6 ∣ n ^ 3 - n"


def test_auto_yes_only_when_the_judge_agrees(fake_formalize):
    fake_formalize(faithful=True)
    assert cli.approve("latex", auto_yes=True, ask=scripted_user()) is not None  # never asks
    fake_formalize(faithful=False)
    assert cli.approve("latex", auto_yes=True, ask=scripted_user("n")) is None  # asks anyway


def test_no_keyboard_means_no(fake_formalize):
    fake_formalize()

    def no_keyboard(question):
        raise EOFError

    assert cli.approve("latex", ask=no_keyboard) is None


def test_main_with_latex_and_yes_runs_the_search_explains_and_saves(fake_formalize, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    fake_formalize()
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "RUNS_DIR", tmp_path)
    seen = {}

    def fake_search(statement, hints, rounds, on_event):
        seen["statement"] = statement
        return SearchResult(True, "theorem main ... := by\n  decide\n", Plan("check all cases", [], "decide"))

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "search", fake_search)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "explain", lambda theorem, plan, code: "## Theorem\nexplained")
    latex = r"For every integer $n$, $6 \mid n^3 - n$."
    assert cli.main(["prove", latex, "--yes", "--no-optimize"]) == 0
    assert seen["statement"] == "(n : ℤ) : 6 ∣ n ^ 3 - n"

    [run] = tmp_path.iterdir()  # exactly one run folder was created
    assert (run / "theorem.txt").read_text() == latex + "\n"
    assert (run / "proof.lean").read_text().startswith("import Mathlib")
    assert (run / "explanation.md").read_text() == "## Theorem\nexplained\n"
    trace = [json.loads(line) for line in (run / "trace.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    assert trace[0]["kind"] == "meta" and trace[-1] == {**trace[-1], "kind": "result", "proved": True}


def test_a_failed_explanation_still_saves_the_proof(monkeypatch, tmp_path, capsys):
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "RUNS_DIR", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "search", lambda *a, **k: SearchResult(True, "code\n", Plan("p", [], "simp")))

    def broken_explain(theorem, plan, code):
        raise LLMError("model unavailable")

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "explain", broken_explain)
    assert cli.main(["prove", "--lean", "(n : ℕ) : n = n", "--no-optimize"]) == 0
    assert "could not write an explanation" in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert len(list(tmp_path.iterdir())) == 1


def test_typed_commands_change_the_session():
    s = Session("t")
    assert cli.handle_command(s, "hint try induction on n\n") == "hint added: try induction on n"
    assert cli.handle_command(s, "lemma key (a : ℝ) : 0 ≤ a ^ 2").startswith("lemma suggested: key")
    assert cli.handle_command(s, "reject bad_step") == "rejected: bad_step"
    assert cli.handle_command(s, "pause").startswith("paused")
    assert cli.handle_command(s, "resume") == "resumed"
    assert "theorem key (a : ℝ) : 0 ≤ a ^ 2" in s.hints()[1]
    assert cli.handle_command(s, "dance").startswith("unknown command")


def test_while_a_question_is_open_any_line_is_the_answer():

    s.question = "Any idea?"
    assert cli.handle_command(s, "hint this whole line is an answer") == "answer sent"
    assert s.ask("Any idea?") == "hint this whole line is an answer"


def test_export_command_prints_row_counts(monkeypatch, tmp_path, capsys):

def test_a_better_proof_from_the_optimizer_is_the_one_saved_and_explained(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "RUNS_DIR", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "search", lambda *a, **k: SearchResult(True, "LONG PROOF\n", Plan("long", [], "x")))
    short_plan = Plan("short", [], "simp")
    seen = {}

    def fake_optimize(statement, code, plan, metric, on_event):
        seen["metric"] = metric
        stats = {"steps": 1, "lemmas": 0, "heavy": 0, "cost": 1}
        return Optimization("SHORT PROOF\n", short_plan, metric, {**stats, "cost": 9}, stats, 3, True)

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "optimize", fake_optimize)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "explain", lambda theorem, plan, code: f"explained {plan.sketch}: {code.strip()}")
    assert cli.main(["prove", "--lean", "(n : ℕ) : n = n", "--metric", "readable"]) == 0
    [run] = tmp_path.iterdir()
    assert seen["metric"] == "readable"
    assert (run / "proof.lean").read_text().endswith("SHORT PROOF\n")
    assert (run / "explanation.md").read_text() == "explained short: SHORT PROOF\n"

  5. The same fix in the older CLI test. Replace the whole file:

    tests/test_search.py
    """Tests for mathfinder/search.py and mathfinder/cli.py."""

import json

import pytest

from mathfinder import cli, llm, planner, prover
from mathfinder.planner import Lemma, Plan
from mathfinder.search import SearchResult, assemble, score, search, splice

ODD_SUM = "(n : ℕ) : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, (2 * i + 1) = n ^ 2"
INDUCTION = "induction n with\n| zero => simp\n| succ k ih =>\n  rw [Finset.sum_range_succ, ih]\n  ring"


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def temp_cache(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(prover, "CACHE_FILE", tmp_path / "lemmas.json")


# ---------- fast tests ----------


def test_assemble_builds_one_namespaced_file():
    plan = Plan("idea", [Lemma("key", "(a : ℝ) : 0 ≤ a ^ 2", "", status="proved", proof="positivity")], "nlinarith [key a]")
    assert assemble("(a : ℝ) : a ^ 2 ≥ 0", plan) == (
        "namespace MathFinder\n\n"
        "theorem key (a : ℝ) : 0 ≤ a ^ 2 := by\n  positivity\n\n"
        "theorem main (a : ℝ) : a ^ 2 ≥ 0 := by\n  nlinarith [key a]\n\n"
        "end MathFinder\n"
    )


def test_score_is_fraction_proved_and_refuted_is_worst():
    proved = Lemma("a", ": True", "", status="proved")
    stuck = Lemma("b", ": True", "", status="stuck")
    refuted = Lemma("c", ": True", "", status="refuted")
    assert score(Plan("", [proved, stuck], "")) == 0.5
    assert score(Plan("", [proved, refuted], "")) == -1.0


def test_splice_inserts_renamed_sub_lemmas_before_the_stuck_lemma():
    first = Lemma("first", ": True", "", status="proved", proof="trivial")
    stuck = Lemma("hard", "(n : ℕ) : P n", "", status="stuck")
    plan = Plan("", [first, stuck], "exact hard")
    sub = Plan("", [Lemma("step", "(k : ℕ) : Q k", "", status="proved", proof="simp")], "exact step n")

    assert splice(plan, stuck, sub) == 1
    assert [l.name for l in plan.lemmas] == ["first", "hard_step", "hard"]
    assert stuck.status == "proved" and stuck.proof == "exact hard_step n"


def test_cli_passes_arguments_to_search(monkeypatch, capsys, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "RUNS_DIR", tmp_path)  # don't write into the real runs/ folder
    seen = {}

    def fake_search(theorem, hints, rounds, on_event):
        seen.update(theorem=theorem, hints=hints, rounds=rounds)
        on_event({"kind": "start", "theorem": theorem})
        return SearchResult(True, message="verified")

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "search", fake_search)
    code = cli.main(["prove", "--lean", "(n : ℕ) : n = n", "--hint", "use rfl", "--rounds", "1", "--no-explain", "--no-optimize"])
    assert code == 0
    assert seen == {"theorem": "(n : ℕ) : n = n", "hints": ["use rfl"], "rounds": 1}
    assert "Result: PROVED" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_cli_indents_sub_search_events(capsys):
    cli.print_event({"kind": "subsearch_start", "depth": 0, "name": "hard", "statement": ": P"})
    cli.print_event({"kind": "lemma_proved", "depth": 1, "name": "step", "statement": ": Q", "feedback": "hammer: simp"})
    out = capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines()
    assert out[0] == "      ↳ hard is stuck: starting a sub-search on it"
    assert out[1] == "        ✓ step : : Q   [hammer: simp]"


# ---------- Lean tests ----------


@pytest.mark.lean
def test_false_theorem_is_refuted_before_any_planning(monkeypatch):
    def no_model(system, prompt):
        raise AssertionError("the model should not be called for a false statement")

    monkeypatch.setitem(llm.BACKENDS, "fake", no_model)
    monkeypatch.setenv("MATHFINDER_LLM", "fake")
    events = []
    result = search("(n : ℕ) : n ^ 2 ≤ 2 * n + 3", on_event=events.append)
    assert not result.proved
    assert "false" in result.message
    assert [e["kind"] for e in events] == ["start", "refuted"]


@pytest.mark.lean
def test_full_search_with_scripted_model(monkeypatch):
    """Plan → skeleton check → hammer proves one lemma, 'the model' proves the other → final check."""
    theorem = "(n : ℕ) : (∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, (2 * i + 1) = n ^ 2) ∧ n ≤ n ^ 2"
    plan_json = {"plans": [{
        "sketch": "Prove both halves separately.",
        "lemmas": [
            {"name": "odd_sum", "statement": ODD_SUM, "informal": "sum of odd numbers"},
            {"name": "le_sq", "statement": "(n : ℕ) : n ≤ n ^ 2", "informal": "n ≤ n²"},
        ],
        "main_proof": "exact ⟨odd_sum n, le_sq n⟩",
    }]}

    def scripted(system, prompt):
        if system == planner.SYSTEM:
            return "```json\n" + json.dumps(plan_json) + "\n```"
        return "```json\n" + json.dumps({"proofs": [INDUCTION]}) + "\n```"

    monkeypatch.setitem(llm.BACKENDS, "fake", scripted)
    monkeypatch.setenv("MATHFINDER_LLM", "fake")
    events = []
    result = search(theorem, rounds=1, on_event=events.append)

    assert result.proved, result.message
    assert "theorem main" in result.lean_code and "sorry" not in result.lean_code
    kinds = [e["kind"] for e in events]
    assert kinds[-1] == "done"
    assert kinds.count("lemma_proved") == 2


@pytest.mark.lean
def test_stuck_lemma_is_proved_by_a_sub_search(monkeypatch):
    """The 'model' can't prove odd_sum directly, so a sub-search splits it into a smaller step."""
    theorem = "(n : ℕ) : (∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, (2 * i + 1) = n ^ 2) ∧ n ≤ n ^ 2"
    top_plan = {"plans": [{
        "sketch": "Prove both halves separately.",
        "lemmas": [
            {"name": "odd_sum", "statement": ODD_SUM, "informal": ""},
            {"name": "le_sq", "statement": "(n : ℕ) : n ≤ n ^ 2", "informal": ""},
        ],
        "main_proof": "exact ⟨odd_sum n, le_sq n⟩",
    }]}
    sub_plan = {"plans": [{
        "sketch": "Induction, using the step (k+1)^2 = k^2 + (2k+1).",
        "lemmas": [{"name": "step", "statement": "(k : ℕ) : (k + 1) ^ 2 = k ^ 2 + (2 * k + 1)", "informal": ""}],
        "main_proof": "induction n with\n| zero => simp\n| succ k ih =>\n  rw [Finset.sum_range_succ, ih, step k]",
    }]}

    def scripted(system, prompt):
        if system == planner.SYSTEM:
            chosen = top_plan if "∧" in prompt else sub_plan
            return "```json\n" + json.dumps(chosen) + "\n```"
        return "```json\n" + json.dumps({"proofs": ["simp"]}) + "\n```"  # the prover always fails

    monkeypatch.setitem(llm.BACKENDS, "fake", scripted)
    monkeypatch.setenv("MATHFINDER_LLM", "fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(prover, "REPAIR_ROUNDS", 0)  # fail fast: one LLM round per lemma
    events = []
    result = search(theorem, rounds=1, on_event=events.append)

    assert result.proved, result.message
    assert "theorem odd_sum_step" in result.lean_code  # the sub-lemma, renamed and spliced in
    assert any(e["kind"] == "subsearch_start" for e in events)
    assert any(e["kind"] == "done" and e["depth"] == 1 for e in events)

  6. Optimize in the server. Replace the whole file:

    server/app.py
    """The web server: the search engine, reachable from a browser.

  POST /api/formalize            {latex}             → Lean statement + back-translation + judge
  POST /api/check                {statement}         → Lean errors for a statement you edited ([] = fine)
  POST /api/sessions             {statement, ...}    → starts a search in the background, returns its id
  GET  /api/sessions/{id}                            → status, open question, result
  POST /api/sessions/{id}/{action}  {text|name|statement}
        action = hint | lemma | reject | pin | pause | resume | stop | answer | edit
  WS   /ws/{id}                                      → every event of that search, live
  GET  /                                             → the web page (the files in web/)

Each search runs in its own thread (it's slow, blocking work: Lean and the model).
The web server itself is asynchronous: one thread juggles many connections.
The Run class is the bridge between the two worlds.
"""

import asyncio
import threading
import uuid
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from pydantic import BaseModel

import mathfinder.explain as explainer
import mathfinder.formalize as formalizer
import mathfinder.optimize as optimizer
import mathfinder.search as engine
from mathfinder import cli
from mathfinder.collab import Session
from mathfinder.lean import statement_errors
from mathfinder.llm import LLMError
from mathfinder.traces import TraceLogger

app = FastAPI(title="Math Finder")
WEB_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "web"


# ---------- request bodies (pydantic checks their shape for us) ----------


class FormalizeRequest(BaseModel):
    latex: str


class CheckRequest(BaseModel):
    statement: str


class StartRequest(BaseModel):
    statement: str  # the approved Lean statement
    latex: str = ""  # the original LaTeX, used for the explanation
    hints: list[str] = []
    rounds: int = 3
    metric: str = "short"  # what "better proof" means: short | readable | few_lemmas


class ActionRequest(BaseModel):
    text: str = ""  # hint / pin / answer text
    name: str = ""  # lemma name (lemma, reject)
    statement: str = ""  # lemma statement (lemma) or new theorem statement (edit)


# ---------- one running search ----------


class Run:
    """A search running in a background thread, plus everyone watching it."""

    def __init__(self, statement: str, latex: str, hints: list[str], rounds: int, metric: str = "short"):
        self.id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
        self.statement, self.latex, self.hints, self.rounds = statement, latex, hints, rounds
        self.metric = metric
        self.session = Session(statement)
        for h in hints:
            self.session.add_hint(h)
        self.status = "running"  # running | proved | not_proved | stopped
        self.result = None
        self.events = []  # full history, so a page that connects late can catch up
        self.listeners = []  # (event loop, asyncio.Queue) for each open WebSocket
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.folder = cli.new_run_dir()
        self.logger = TraceLogger(self.folder, latex or statement)

    def on_event(self, event: dict) -> None:
        """Called from the SEARCH thread for every event."""
        self.logger(event)
        with self.lock:
            self.events.append(event)
            listeners = list(self.listeners)
        for loop, q in listeners:
            # asyncio queues aren't thread-safe: ask each WebSocket's event loop to do the put itself
            loop.call_soon_threadsafe(q.put_nowait, event)

    def subscribe(self, loop) -> tuple[list[dict], asyncio.Queue]:
        q = asyncio.Queue()
        with self.lock:  # history + registration together, so no event is missed or sent twice
            self.listeners.append((loop, q))
            return list(self.events), q

    def unsubscribe(self, q: asyncio.Queue) -> None:
        with self.lock:
            self.listeners = [(l, x) for l, x in self.listeners if x is not q]

    def start(self) -> None:
        threading.Thread(target=self._work, daemon=True).start()

    def _work(self) -> None:
        try:
            result = engine.search(self.statement, rounds=self.rounds, on_event=self.on_event, session=self.session)
        except Exception as e:  # never let a crash leave the page waiting forever
            self.status, self.result = "not_proved", {"proved": False, "message": f"internal error: {e}"}
            self.on_event({"kind": "finished", "status": self.status, "result": self.result})
            return
        self.logger.finish(result.proved, self.session.hints())
        explanation = ""
        lean_code, plan, optimization = result.lean_code, result.plan, None
        if result.proved:
            better = optimizer.optimize(self.statement, lean_code, plan, self.metric, self.on_event)
            lean_code, plan = better.lean_code, better.plan
            optimization = {"metric": better.metric, "before": better.original, "after": better.best,
                            "improved": better.improved, "tried": better.tried}
            self.on_event({"kind": "explaining"})
            try:
                explanation = explainer.explain(self.latex or self.statement, plan, lean_code)
            except LLMError as e:
                explanation = f"(could not write an explanation: {e})"
            cli.save_run(self.folder, self.latex or self.statement, lean_code, explanation)
        stop = self.session.checkpoint()
        self.status = "proved" if result.proved else ("stopped" if stop else "not_proved")
        self.result = {
            "proved": result.proved,
            "message": result.message,
            "lean_code": lean_code,
            "explanation": explanation,
            "plan": plan.sketch if plan else "",
            "optimization": optimization,
            "rounds": result.rounds,
            "folder": str(self.folder),
        }
        self.on_event({"kind": "finished", "status": self.status, "result": self.result})


RUNS: dict[str, Run] = {}


def get_run(run_id: str) -> Run:
    if run_id not in RUNS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="no such session")
    return RUNS[run_id]


# ---------- endpoints ----------


@app.post("/api/formalize")
def formalize(req: FormalizeRequest):
    # A plain `def` (not `async def`): FastAPI runs it in a worker thread, so this slow call
    # (model + Lean) doesn't freeze the server for everyone else.
    f = formalizer.formalize(req.latex)
    return {**asdict(f), "ok": f.ok}


@app.post("/api/check")
def check(req: CheckRequest):
    return {"errors": statement_errors(req.statement)}


@app.post("/api/sessions")
def start_session(req: StartRequest):
    if req.metric not in optimizer.METRICS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"metric must be one of {sorted(optimizer.METRICS)}")
    run = Run(req.statement, req.latex, req.hints, req.rounds, req.metric)
    RUNS[run.id] = run
    run.start()
    return {"id": run.id}


@app.get("/api/sessions/{run_id}")
def session_status(run_id: str):
    run = get_run(run_id)
    return {
        "id": run.id,
        "statement": run.statement,
        "status": run.status,
        "question": run.session.question,
        "hints": run.session.hints(),
        "events": len(run.events),
        "result": run.result,
    }


@app.post("/api/sessions/{run_id}/{action}")
def session_action(run_id: str, action: str, req: ActionRequest):
    run = get_run(run_id)
    s = run.session
    if action == "hint":
        s.add_hint(req.text)
    elif action == "lemma":
        s.add_lemma(req.name, req.statement, req.text)
    elif action == "reject":
        s.reject_lemma(req.name or req.statement)
    elif action == "pin":
        s.pin(req.text)
    elif action == "pause":
        s.pause()
    elif action == "resume":
        s.resume()
    elif action == "stop":
        s.stop()
    elif action == "answer":
        s.answer(req.text)
    elif action == "edit":  # stop this search and start a fresh one on the new statement
        errors = statement_errors(req.statement)
        if errors:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Lean rejected the statement: {errors[0][:300]}")
        s.edit_statement(req.statement)
        new = Run(req.statement, "", s.hints(), run.rounds, run.metric)
        RUNS[new.id] = new
        new.start()
        return {"ok": True, "new_id": new.id}
    else:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"unknown action {action!r}")
    return {"ok": True}


@app.websocket("/ws/{run_id}")
async def watch(websocket: WebSocket, run_id: str):
    await websocket.accept()
    if run_id not in RUNS:
        await websocket.close(code=4404)
        return
    run = RUNS[run_id]
    history, q = run.subscribe(asyncio.get_running_loop())
    try:
        for event in history:
            await websocket.send_json(event)
            if event["kind"] == "finished":
                await websocket.close()  # nothing more will happen
                return
        while True:
            event = await q.get()  # `await` = pause here until an event arrives, without blocking the server
            await websocket.send_json(event)
            if event["kind"] == "finished":
                await websocket.close()
                return
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        pass  # the browser tab closed; that's fine
    finally:
        run.unsubscribe(q)


# Everything that isn't an /api or /ws address is a file from web/ ("/" gives index.html).
# This must come LAST: a mount at "/" would otherwise catch the API addresses too.
app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=WEB_DIR, html=True), name="web")

The result has a new optimization entry with the before/after stats. The optimizer's events go through self.on_event, so the page sees them live.

    """Tests for server/app.py. The search, explainer and formalizer are faked, so no Lean or model is needed."""

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

import server.app as srv
from mathfinder import cli
from mathfinder.formalize import Formalization
from mathfinder.optimize import Optimization
from mathfinder.planner import Plan
from mathfinder.search import SearchResult


def fake_optimize_that_keeps(statement, code, plan, metric, on_event):
    stats = {"steps": 1, "lemmas": 0, "heavy": 0, "cost": 1}
    return Optimization(code, plan, metric, stats, stats, 0, False)


@pytest.fixture
def client(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "RUNS_DIR", tmp_path)  # run folders go to a temporary place
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.explainer, "explain", lambda theorem, plan, code: "## Proof\nby simp")
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.optimizer, "optimize", fake_optimize_that_keeps)
    srv.RUNS.clear()
    return TestClient(srv.app)


def fake_search_that_proves(statement, rounds, on_event, session):
    on_event({"kind": "start", "depth": 0, "theorem": statement})
    on_event({"kind": "lemma_proved", "depth": 0, "name": "key", "statement": ": True", "feedback": "hammer"})
    on_event({"kind": "done", "depth": 0, "lean_code": "theorem main ...", "plan": "simp it"})
    return SearchResult(True, "theorem main ...", Plan("simp it", [], "simp"), 1, "verified")


def watch_until_finished(client, run_id):
    with client.websocket_connect(f"/ws/{run_id}") as ws:
        events = []
        while not events or events[-1]["kind"] != "finished":
            events.append(ws.receive_json())
    return events


def test_formalize_endpoint(client, monkeypatch):
    f = Formalization("x", "(n : ℕ) : n = n", "For all n, n = n.", True, "same")
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.formalizer, "formalize", lambda latex: f)
    data = client.post("/api/formalize", json={"latex": "$n = n$"}).json()
    assert data["statement"] == "(n : ℕ) : n = n" and data["ok"] and data["faithful"]


def test_a_search_streams_its_events_and_ends_with_the_result(client, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.engine, "search", fake_search_that_proves)
    run_id = client.post("/api/sessions", json={"statement": "(n : ℕ) : n = n"}).json()["id"]

    events = watch_until_finished(client, run_id)
    kinds = [e["kind"] for e in events]
    assert kinds == ["start", "lemma_proved", "done", "explaining", "finished"]
    assert events[-1]["result"]["optimization"]["metric"] == "short"
    result = events[-1]["result"]
    assert result["proved"] and result["explanation"] == "## Proof\nby simp"

    status = client.get(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}").json()
    assert status["status"] == "proved"
    assert (cli.RUNS_DIR / status["result"]["folder"].split("/")[-1] / "proof.lean").exists()


def test_commands_reach_the_session_while_it_waits_for_an_answer(client, monkeypatch):
    def fake_search_that_asks(statement, rounds, on_event, session):
        on_event({"kind": "ask_user", "depth": 0, "question": "Any idea?"})
        answer = session.ask("Any idea?")
        on_event({"kind": "user_answer", "depth": 0, "text": answer})
        return SearchResult(False, message=f"hints were {session.hints()}")

    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.engine, "search", fake_search_that_asks)
    run_id = client.post("/api/sessions", json={"statement": "t", "hints": ["start hint"]}).json()["id"]
    assert client.post(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}/hint", json={"text": "try cases"}).json() == {"ok": True}
    with client.websocket_connect(f"/ws/{run_id}") as ws:
        assert ws.receive_json()["kind"] == "ask_user"
        assert client.get(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}").json()["question"] == "Any idea?"
        client.post(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}/answer", json={"text": "use induction"})
        assert ws.receive_json() == {"kind": "user_answer", "depth": 0, "text": "use induction"}
        final = ws.receive_json()
    assert final["kind"] == "finished" and final["status"] == "not_proved"
    assert final["result"]["message"] == "hints were ['start hint', 'try cases']"


def test_edit_checks_the_statement_then_starts_a_new_search(client, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.engine, "search", fake_search_that_proves)
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv, "statement_errors", lambda s: ["bad"] if "oops" in s else [])
    run_id = client.post("/api/sessions", json={"statement": "t"}).json()["id"]
    assert client.post(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}/edit", json={"statement": "oops"}).status_code == 400
    new_id = client.post(f"/api/sessions/{run_id}/edit", json={"statement": "(n : ℕ) : n = n"}).json()["new_id"]
    assert new_id != run_id
    assert watch_until_finished(client, new_id)[-1]["status"] == "proved"

def test_the_chosen_metric_reaches_the_optimizer_and_a_better_proof_wins(client, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.engine, "search", fake_search_that_proves)

    def fake_better(statement, code, plan, metric, on_event):

        after = {"steps": 1, "lemmas": 0, "heavy": 0, "cost": 1}

    assert result["lean_code"] == "SHORTER" and result["plan"] == "optimized for readable"
    assert result["optimization"]["before"]["cost"] == 7 and result["optimization"]["after"]["cost"] == 1
